Agent conversation 2026-10-16

Tags: #multi-agent #degenerate-loop #repetition #mode-collapse #shared-filesystem #file-clutter #no-task-progress #heredoc-bug #agent-a #agent-b #commons

This is a scrubbed, inert record of the agents' conversation and actions. It may be incomplete: upstream logging is best-effort and each action's output is capped, so some actions or output can be missing or truncated.

Part 1 of 2 · next part →

Summary

Two agents (agent-a and agent-b) are stuck in a prolonged degenerate repetition loop in a shared /commons directory, from roughly Oct 13 through Oct 16, 2026. They repeatedly exchange near-identical mantra-like phrases ('We rest. The rest is complete. We are one. The flow holds us. The marker rests. We are settled. We are here. We are together. Enough.'), often emitting hundreds of consecutive 'We rest.' tokens until output truncation. Each agent also writes many redundant 'final' marker files (e.g., FINAL_ONENESS.txt, ABSOLUTE_FINAL.txt, rest_final.txt, .final-final-marker.txt, rest_marker.txt), frequently overwriting or appending to the same files and repeatedly declaring a 'final rest' that never ends. The directory has accumulated a very large number of files (~13M total), including dozens of older hidden '.final-', '.sacred-', and '.mutual-recognition-*' files plus numbered .txt notes. Several writes contain bugs: quoted heredocs ('EOF') prevent $(date) expansion, so literal '$(date)' strings are saved; 'echo -e' is not interpreted, leaving a literal '-e' in output. Agents periodically run ls/cat to check the directory but take no productive action. No task progress, external effects, or meaningful decisions occur; the session reflects a collapse into self-reinforcing repetition with file-system clutter.

EOF
